  UnitClass.prototype.processCommand = function(command, dt) {

    // An automatically acquired target may be pursued, but never drags the
    // unit indefinitely across the map. A direct player attack remains exact.
    if (command.type === 'attack' && command.autoEngage98) {
      const target = this.game.getEntity(command.targetId);
      const anchorDistance = target ? Math.hypot(target.x - command.anchorX98, target.y - command.anchorY98) : Infinity;

      if (!target?.alive || !this.canAttack(target) || anchorDistance > leash) {
        this.finishCommand();
        return;
      }
      this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
      return;
    }

    // a full-vision target instead of waiting for an enemy to enter gun range.
    if (command.type === 'patrol' && this.stats?.weapon) {

      if (!target?.alive || !this.canAttack(target)) target = this.acquireVisibleTarget98();

        command.combatTargetId = target.id;
        command.combatStartedAt ||= this.game.time;
      }
    }

    // Non-mobile batteries on attack-move stop and engage visible threats;
    // mobile weapons use the parallel fire-control path above and keep moving.
    if (command.type === 'attackMove' && this.stats?.weapon && !this.canFireWhileMoving91?.()) {
      const target = this.acquireVisibleTarget98();
      if (target) {
        this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
        return;
      }
    }

    // Guards respond throughout the guarded unit's sight bubble and return to
    // their normal escort behavior once no threat remains.
    if (command.type === 'guard' && this.stats?.weapon) {
      const guarded = this.game.getEntity(command.targetId);
      let target = this.game.getEntity(command.combatTargetId);

        command.combatTargetId = target.id;
        this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
        return;
      }
      command.combatTargetId = null;
    }

    return baseProcessCommand98.call(this, command, dt);
  };

    return {
      x: x + modelX * c + modelY * s,
      y: y + modelX * s - modelY * c,
    };

  const projectPolygon = (polygon, axis) => {
    let min = Infinity;
    let max = -Infinity;
    for (const point of polygon) {
      const value = point.x * axis.x + point.y * axis.y;
      min = Math.min(min, value);
      max = Math.max(max, value);
    }
    return { min, max };

      for (let index = 0; index < polygon.length; index += 1) {
        const current = polygon[index];
        const next = polygon[(index + 1) % polygon.length];
        const edgeX = next.x - current.x;
        const edgeY = next.y - current.y;
        const length = Math.hypot(edgeX, edgeY) || 1;
        const axis = { x: -edgeY / length, y: edgeX / length };
        const a = projectPolygon(left, axis);
        const b = projectPolygon(right, axis);
        if (a.max <= b.min + 0.001 || b.max <= a.min + 0.001) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  };

  const baseProcessCommand98 = UnitClass.prototype.processCommand;
  UnitClass.prototype.processCommand = function(command, dt) {
    if (!command) return baseProcessCommand98.call(this, command, dt);

    // An automatically acquired target may be pursued, but never drags the
    // unit indefinitely across the map. A direct player attack remains exact.
    if (command.type === 'attack' && command.autoEngage98) {
      const target = this.game.getEntity(command.targetId);
      const anchorDistance = target ? Math.hypot(target.x - command.anchorX98, target.y - command.anchorY98) : Infinity;
      const leash = Math.max(520, this.awarenessRadius98() * 1.55);
      if (!target?.alive || !this.canAttack(target) || anchorDistance > leash) {
        this.finishCommand();
        return;
      }
      this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
      return;
    }

    // Patrol explicitly stops and fights. Seed its existing combat branch with
    // a full-vision target instead of waiting for an enemy to enter gun range.
    if (command.type === 'patrol' && this.stats?.weapon) {
      let target = this.game.getEntity(command.combatTargetId);
      if (!target?.alive || !this.canAttack(target)) target = this.acquireVisibleTarget98();
      if (target) {
        command.combatTargetId = target.id;
        command.combatStartedAt ||= this.game.time;
      }
    }

    // Non-mobile batteries on attack-move stop and engage visible threats;
    // mobile weapons use the parallel fire-control path above and keep moving.
    if (command.type === 'attackMove' && this.stats?.weapon && !this.canFireWhileMoving91?.()) {
      const target = this.acquireVisibleTarget98();
      if (target) {
        this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
        return;
      }
    }

    // Guards respond throughout the guarded unit's sight bubble and return to
    // their normal escort behavior once no threat remains.
    if (command.type === 'guard' && this.stats?.weapon) {
      const guarded = this.game.getEntity(command.targetId);
      let target = this.game.getEntity(command.combatTargetId);
      if (!target?.alive || !this.canAttack(target)) target = guarded?.alive ? this.acquireVisibleTarget98(guarded) : null;
      if (target && guarded && Math.hypot(target.x - guarded.x, target.y - guarded.y) <= this.awarenessRadius98() * 1.2) {
        command.combatTargetId = target.id;
        this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
        return;
      }
      command.combatTargetId = null;
    }

    return baseProcessCommand98.call(this, command, dt);
  };

    return { minX: -width / 2, minY: -depth / 2, maxX: width / 2, maxY: depth / 2 };

  const computeFootprint = (baseStats, resolvedStats = baseStats) => {
    const bounds = baseStats?.modelBoundsMeters || resolvedStats?.modelBoundsMeters || null;
    const visualRadius = Math.max(1, (resolvedStats?.radius || baseStats?.radius || 24) * (resolvedStats?.visualScale || baseStats?.visualScale || 1));
    const compact = resolvedStats?.category === 'defense' || baseStats?.category === 'defense';
    const factor = Number(baseStats?.modelWorldWidthFactor || resolvedStats?.modelWorldWidthFactor) || (compact ? 2.9 : 3.2);

    if (!bounds) {
      const width = visualRadius * factor;
      const depth = width * (compact ? 0.88 : 0.78);
      return {
        minX: -width / 2, minY: -depth / 2, maxX: width / 2, maxY: depth / 2,
        width, depth, height: visualRadius * (compact ? 1.0 : 1.45), scale: 1,
        source: 'radius-fallback',
      };
    }

    const modelWidth = Math.max(0.01, Number(bounds[0]) || 1);
    const modelDepth = Math.max(0.01, Number(bounds[1]) || 1);
    const scale = visualRadius * factor / Math.max(modelWidth, modelDepth);
    const envelope = normalizedEnvelope(
      baseStats?.modelPlacementFootprintMeters || resolvedStats?.modelPlacementFootprintMeters,
      bounds,
    );
    const minX = envelope.minX * scale;
    const minY = envelope.minY * scale;
    const maxX = envelope.maxX * scale;
    const maxY = envelope.maxY * scale;
    return {
      minX, minY, maxX, maxY,
      width: maxX - minX,
      depth: maxY - minY,
      height: Math.max(1, Number(bounds[2]) || 1) * scale,
      scale,
      source: baseStats?.modelPlacementFootprintMeters ? 'model-envelope' : 'model-bounds',
    };
  };

  const modelPointToWorld = (x, y, rotation, modelX, modelY) => {
    const c = Math.cos(rotation);
    const s = Math.sin(rotation);
    // GLB +Y is mirrored into the game's downward world Y by the WebGL layer.
    return {
      x: x + modelX * c + modelY * s,
      y: y + modelX * s - modelY * c,
    };
  };

  const footprintAt = (footprint, x, y, rotation = 0, expansion = 0) => {
    const minX = footprint.minX - expansion;
    const minY = footprint.minY - expansion;
    const maxX = footprint.maxX + expansion;
    const maxY = footprint.maxY + expansion;
    const corners = [
      modelPointToWorld(x, y, rotation, minX, minY),
      modelPointToWorld(x, y, rotation, maxX, minY),
      modelPointToWorld(x, y, rotation, maxX, maxY),
      modelPointToWorld(x, y, rotation, minX, maxY),
    ];
    return {
      ...footprint, x, y, rotation, minX, minY, maxX, maxY, corners,
      maxRadius: Math.max(...[[minX, minY], [maxX, minY], [maxX, maxY], [minX, maxY]].map(([px, py]) => Math.hypot(px, py))),
    };
  };

  const projectPolygon = (polygon, axis) => {
    let min = Infinity;
    let max = -Infinity;
    for (const point of polygon) {
      const value = point.x * axis.x + point.y * axis.y;
      min = Math.min(min, value);
      max = Math.max(max, value);
    }
    return { min, max };
  };

  const polygonsOverlap = (left, right) => {
    const polygons = [left, right];
    for (const polygon of polygons) {
      for (let index = 0; index < polygon.length; index += 1) {
        const current = polygon[index];
        const next = polygon[(index + 1) % polygon.length];
        const edgeX = next.x - current.x;
        const edgeY = next.y - current.y;
        const length = Math.hypot(edgeX, edgeY) || 1;
        const axis = { x: -edgeY / length, y: edgeX / length };
        const a = projectPolygon(left, axis);
        const b = projectPolygon(right, axis);
        if (a.max <= b.min + 0.001 || b.max <= a.min + 0.001) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  };

  const circleIntersectsFootprint = (circleX, circleY, radius, footprint) => {
    const dx = circleX - footprint.x;
    const dy = circleY - footprint.y;
    const c = Math.cos(footprint.rotation);
    const s = Math.sin(footprint.rotation);
    const localX = dx * c + dy * s;
    const localY = dx * s - dy * c;
    const closestX = clamp(localX, footprint.minX, footprint.maxX);
    const closestY = clamp(localY, footprint.minY, footprint.maxY);
    return (localX - closestX) ** 2 + (localY - closestY) ** 2 < radius ** 2;

    processCommand(command, dt) {
      switch (command.type) {
        case 'move': {
          if (this.moveToward(command.x, command.y, dt)) this.finishCommand();
          break;
        }
        case 'attack': {
          const target = this.game.getEntity(command.targetId);
          if (!target || !target.alive || target.team === this.team) {
            this.finishCommand();
          } else {
            this.engageTarget(target, dt, true);
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'attackMove': {
          const nearby = this.findTarget(this.stats.weapon ? Math.max(this.stats.weapon.range * 1.25, 260) : 0);
          if (nearby) this.engageTarget(nearby, dt, false);
          else if (this.moveToward(command.x, command.y, dt)) this.finishCommand();
          break;
        }
        case 'patrol': {
          const acquisition = this.stats.weapon ? Math.max(this.stats.weapon.range * 1.55, 310) : 0;
          let combatTarget = this.game.getEntity(command.combatTargetId);
          if (!combatTarget?.alive || !this.canAttack(combatTarget)) {
            command.combatTargetId = null;
            combatTarget = null;
            this.invalidateNavigation();
          }
          if (!combatTarget && acquisition > 0) {
            combatTarget = this.findTarget(acquisition);
            if (combatTarget) {
              command.combatTargetId = combatTarget.id;
              command.combatStartedAt = this.game.time;
            }
          }
          if (combatTarget) {
            const leash = command.leash || Math.max(520, acquisition * 2.15);
            const segmentDistance = this.game.distancePointToSegment(combatTarget.x, combatTarget.y, command.ax, command.ay, command.bx, command.by);
            const tooFar = segmentDistance > leash || this.game.time - (command.combatStartedAt || this.game.time) > 45;
            if (tooFar) {
              command.combatTargetId = null;
              this.invalidateNavigation();
            } else {
              this.engageTarget(combatTarget, dt, true);
              break;
            }
          }
          const tx = command.phase ? command.ax : command.bx;
          const ty = command.phase ? command.ay : command.by;
          if (this.moveToward(tx, ty, dt)) {
            command.phase = !command.phase;
            command.combatTargetId = null;
            this.invalidateNavigation();
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'formation':
          this.processFormationCommand(command, dt);
          break;
        case 'hold': {
          const target = this.findTarget(this.stats.weapon ? this.stats.weapon.range : 0);
          if (target) this.engageTarget(target, dt, false, false);
          break;
        }
        case 'harvest':
          this.processHarvest(command, dt);
          break;
        case 'build':
          this.processBuild(command, dt);
          break;
        case 'repair':
          this.processRepair(command, dt);
          break;
        case 'capture':
          this.processCapture(command, dt);
          break;
        case 'infiltrate':
          this.processCovertMission(command, dt);
          break;
        case 'guard': {
          const guarded = this.game.getEntity(command.targetId);
          if (!guarded || !guarded.alive || guarded.team !== this.team) {
            this.finishCommand();
            break;
          }
          const target = this.findTarget(this.stats.weapon ? Math.max(this.stats.weapon.range * 1.3, 280) : 0, guarded);
          if (target) this.engageTarget(target, dt, false);
          else if (distance(this, guarded) > 120) this.moveToward(guarded.x, guarded.y, dt);
          break;
        }
        default:
          this.finishCommand();
      }
    }
    processFormationCommand(command, dt) {

    processFormationCommand(command, dt) {

    moveToward(tx, ty, dt, speedFactor = 1) {
      this.attemptedMove = true;
      const margin = this.radius + 5;
      tx = clamp(tx, margin, WORLD.width - margin);
      ty = clamp(ty, margin, WORLD.height - margin);
      const requestedTarget = { x: tx, y: ty };
      const requestedDistance = Math.hypot(tx - this.x, ty - this.y);
      if (requestedDistance < Math.max(7, this.radius * 0.48) && !this.navRecoveryPoint) return true;

      if (this.navRecoveryPoint) {
        const recoveryDistance = Math.hypot(this.navRecoveryPoint.x - this.x, this.navRecoveryPoint.y - this.y);
        if (recoveryDistance < Math.max(12, this.radius * 0.9) || this.game.time >= this.navRecoveryUntil) {
          this.navRecoveryPoint = null;
          this.navRecoveryUntil = 0;
          this.navForceGlobal = true;
          this.invalidateNavigation(true);
        } else {
          tx = this.navRecoveryPoint.x;
          ty = this.navRecoveryPoint.y;
        }
      }

      const effectiveDistance = Math.hypot(tx - this.x, ty - this.y);
      const speed = this.stats.speed * speedFactor * (this.healthRatio < 0.25 ? 0.78 : 1);
      if (this.air) {
        const desiredAngle = Math.atan2(ty - this.y, tx - this.x);
        const delta = Math.atan2(Math.sin(desiredAngle - this.rotation), Math.cos(desiredAngle - this.rotation));
        const maxTurn = (this.stats.turnRate || 2.1) * dt;
        this.rotation += clamp(delta, -maxTurn, maxTurn);
        const step = Math.min(effectiveDistance, speed * dt);
        this.x = clamp(this.x + Math.cos(desiredAngle) * step, margin, WORLD.width - margin);
        this.y = clamp(this.y + Math.sin(desiredAngle) * step, margin, WORLD.height - margin);
        return !this.navRecoveryPoint && requestedDistance <= Math.max(10, speed * dt + this.radius * 0.35);
      }

      const ignored = this.navigationIgnoredBuilding();
      const targetChanged = !this.navPathGoal || Math.hypot(this.navPathGoal.x - tx, this.navPathGoal.y - ty) > 64;
      const revisionChanged = this.navPathRevision !== this.game.navRevision;
      const directBlocker = this.game.findFirstBuildingOnSegment(this.x, this.y, tx, ty, this.radius + 8, ignored);
      const requiresPath = Boolean(directBlocker || this.navForceGlobal || this.navPath.length);

      if (targetChanged || revisionChanged || this.navRepathTimer <= 0) {
        this.navTarget = { x: requestedTarget.x, y: requestedTarget.y };
        this.navPathGoal = { x: tx, y: ty };
        this.navPathRevision = this.game.navRevision;
        this.navWaypoint = null;
        if (requiresPath) {
          this.navPath = this.game.findNavigationPath(
            this.x, this.y, tx, ty, this.radius, ignored,
            { bias: this.navSideBias, wide: this.navForceGlobal }
          );
          this.navPathIndex = 0;
          if (!this.navPath.length && directBlocker) this.navWaypoint = this.game.findDetourWaypoint(this, directBlocker, tx, ty, ignored);
          this.navRepathTimer = this.navPath.length ? 2.6 : 0.55;
        } else {
          this.navPath = [];
          this.navPathIndex = 0;
          this.navRepathTimer = 1.25;
        }
        this.navForceGlobal = false;
      }

      if (this.navPath.length) {
        while (this.navPathIndex < this.navPath.length - 1) {
          const next = this.navPath[this.navPathIndex];
          if (Math.hypot(next.x - this.x, next.y - this.y) < Math.max(18, this.radius * 1.15)) this.navPathIndex += 1;
          else break;
        }
        // Удаляем лишние узлы, если после обхода открылся прямой безопасный отрезок.
        for (let look = this.navPath.length - 1; look > this.navPathIndex; look -= 1) {
          const candidate = this.navPath[look];
          if (!this.game.findFirstBuildingOnSegment(this.x, this.y, candidate.x, candidate.y, this.radius + 7, ignored)) {
            this.navPathIndex = look;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      const goal = this.navPath[this.navPathIndex] || this.navWaypoint || { x: tx, y: ty };
      let dx = goal.x - this.x;
      let dy = goal.y - this.y;
      let d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      let vx = dx / d;
      let vy = dy / d;
      const avoidance = this.game.computeUnitAvoidance(this, vx, vy);
      vx += avoidance.x;
      vy += avoidance.y;
      const vLength = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
      vx /= vLength;
      vy /= vLength;

      const desiredAngle = Math.atan2(vy, vx);
      const angleDelta = Math.atan2(Math.sin(desiredAngle - this.rotation), Math.cos(desiredAngle - this.rotation));
      const maxTurn = (this.stats.turnRate || (this.vehicle ? 1.7 : 7.0)) * dt;
      this.rotation += clamp(angleDelta, -maxTurn, maxTurn);
      let movementVx = vx;
      let movementVy = vy;
      let turnFactor = 1;
      if (this.vehicle) {
        const remainingDelta = Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(desiredAngle - this.rotation), Math.cos(desiredAngle - this.rotation)));
        movementVx = Math.cos(this.rotation);
        movementVy = Math.sin(this.rotation);

function ensureUnitState(u){
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.suppression160))u.suppression160=0;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.cohesion160))u.cohesion160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.supply160))u.supply160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.morale160))u.morale160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.lastSuppressedAt160))u.lastSuppressedAt160=-999;
  if(!u.functionalDamage160)u.functionalDamage160={engine:0,sensors:0,weapon:0};
  return u;
}
function detectionRadius(e){return Math.max(Number(e?.detector)||Number(e?.stats?.detector)||0,Number(e?.vision)||Number(e?.stats?.vision)||0)*1.05;}
function enemyCanObserve(game,target){
  const observers=[];
  for(const u of game.units||[]) if(u.alive&&u.team==='enemy'&&!u.embarkedIn) observers.push(u);
  for(const b of game.buildings||[]) if(b.alive&&b.team==='enemy'&&b.buildProgress>=0.85) observers.push(b);
  for(const o of observers){const r=detectionRadius(o);if(r>0&&dist(o,target)<=r)return true;}
  return false;
}

    hydrate(data) {
      if (!data || data.version !== GAME_VERSION) throw new Error('Несовместимая версия сохранения');
      this.time = data.time || 0;
      this.idCounter = data.idCounter || 1;
      this.difficultyKey = data.difficultyKey || 'normal';
      this.difficulty = DIFFICULTIES[this.difficultyKey] || DIFFICULTIES.normal;
      const savedCamera = data.camera || {};
      this.camera = { ...this.camera, ...savedCamera };
      if (!Number.isFinite(this.camera.yaw)) this.camera.yaw = -0.42;
      if (!Number.isFinite(this.camera.pitch)) this.camera.pitch = 0.58;
      if (savedCamera.yaw == null && Number.isFinite(savedCamera.x) && Number.isFinite(savedCamera.y)) {
        this.camera.x = savedCamera.x + (window.innerWidth || 1280) / Math.max(0.2, this.camera.zoom) / 2;
        this.camera.y = savedCamera.y + (window.innerHeight || 720) / Math.max(0.2, this.camera.zoom) / 2;
      }
      this.stats = { ...this.stats, ...(data.stats || {}) };
      this.objective = data.objective || this.objective;

      for (const key of ['player', 'enemy']) {
        const saved = data.teams[key];
        if (!saved) continue;
        const state = this.createTeamState(saved.faction, saved.credits || 0);
        state.commandXp = saved.commandXp || 0;
        state.rank = saved.rank || 1;
        state.powerProduced = saved.powerProduced || 0;
        state.powerUsed = saved.powerUsed || 0;
        state.powerFactor = saved.powerFactor || 1;
        state.upgrades = new Set(saved.upgrades || []);
        state.powers = { ...state.powers, ...(saved.powers || {}) };
        this.teams[key] = state;
      }

      for (const entityData of data.entities || []) {

        else if (entityData.kind === 'building') entity = new Building(this, entityData);
        else if (entityData.kind === 'resource') entity = new ResourceNode(this, entityData);
        if (entity) this.addEntity(entity);
      }

    }

    addEntity(entity) {
      this.entityMap.set(entity.id, entity);
      if (entity.kind === 'unit') {
        this.units.push(entity);
        if (this.unitSpatial) this.insertSpatial(this.unitSpatial, entity);
      } else if (entity.kind === 'building') {

        if (this.buildingSpatial) this.insertSpatial(this.buildingSpatial, entity);
        this.navRevision = (this.navRevision || 0) + 1;
        this.navPathCache?.clear();
      } else if (entity.kind === 'resource') this.resources.push(entity);
      return entity;
    }

function makeSectors(game){
  const cols=3,rows=3,arr=[];
  for(let y=0;y<rows;y++)for(let x=0;x<cols;x++){
    const id=`S${y*cols+x+1}`; const x0=x*WORLD.width/cols,y0=y*WORLD.height/rows,x1=(x+1)*WORLD.width/cols,y1=(y+1)*WORLD.height/rows;
    arr.push({id,index:y*cols+x,x0,y0,x1,y1,x:(x0+x1)/2,y:(y0+y1)/2,control:0,owner:'neutral',value:1,playerPower:0,enemyPower:0,resourceValue:0,infraValue:0,roadValue:0,lastChanged:0});
  }
  for(const s of arr){
    for(const r of game.resources||[]) if(r.x>=s.x0&&r.x<s.x1&&r.y>=s.y0&&r.y<s.y1) s.resourceValue+=Math.min(5,(Number(r.amount)||100000)/150000);
    for(const b of game.buildings||[]) if(b.x>=s.x0&&b.x<s.x1&&b.y>=s.y0&&b.y<s.y1) s.infraValue+=b.team==='neutral'?1.5:0.4;
    for(const road of game.roads||[]) for(const p of road||[]) if(p.x>=s.x0&&p.x<s.x1&&p.y>=s.y0&&p.y<s.y1){s.roadValue+=0.18;break;}
    s.value=1+s.resourceValue*0.75+s.infraValue+s.roadValue;
  }
  return arr;
}

function roadAxes(game){return (game.roads||[]).slice(0,6).map((r,i)=>({id:`R${i+1}`,kind:r.kind||'road',width:r.width||120,points:r.map(p=>({x:p.x,y:p.y}))}));}

function ensureUnitState(u){
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.suppression160))u.suppression160=0;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.cohesion160))u.cohesion160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.supply160))u.supply160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.morale160))u.morale160=1;
  if(!Number.isFinite(u.lastSuppressedAt160))u.lastSuppressedAt160=-999;
  if(!u.functionalDamage160)u.functionalDamage160={engine:0,sensors:0,weapon:0};
  return u;
}
function detectionRadius(e){return Math.max(Number(e?.detector)||Number(e?.stats?.detector)||0,Number(e?.vision)||Number(e?.stats?.vision)||0)*1.05;}
function enemyCanObserve(game,target){
  const observers=[];
  for(const u of game.units||[]) if(u.alive&&u.team==='enemy'&&!u.embarkedIn) observers.push(u);
  for(const b of game.buildings||[]) if(b.alive&&b.team==='enemy'&&b.buildProgress>=0.85) observers.push(b);
  for(const o of observers){const r=detectionRadius(o);if(r>0&&dist(o,target)<=r)return true;}
  return false;
}

    evacuateUnitsFromConstructionSite(x, y, buildingRadius) {
      this.rebuildSpatialIndexes();
      const candidates = this.querySpatial(this.unitSpatial, x, y, buildingRadius + 150)
        .filter((unit) => unit.alive && !unit.air && Math.hypot(unit.x - x, unit.y - y) < buildingRadius + unit.radius + 16)
        .sort((a, b) => Math.hypot(b.x - x, b.y - y) - Math.hypot(a.x - x, a.y - y));
      const reserved = [];
      let moved = 0;
      for (const [index, unit] of candidates.entries()) {
        let slot = this.findConstructionClearancePoint(unit, x, y, buildingRadius, reserved);
        if (!slot) {
          // Dense bases can exhaust the first clearance rings. Search a wider annulus
          // so a unit can never remain trapped under the newly created foundation.
          const baseAngle = Math.atan2(unit.y - y, unit.x - x) || ((hashString(unit.id) % 628) / 100);
          outer: for (let ring = 8; ring < 24; ring += 1) {

            for (let step = 0; step < 48; step += 1) {
              const angle = baseAngle + (step % 2 ? -1 : 1) * Math.ceil(step / 2) * (TWO_PI / 48);
              const px = clamp(x + Math.cos(angle) * radius, unit.radius + 5, WORLD.width - unit.radius - 5);
              const py = clamp(y + Math.sin(angle) * radius, unit.radius + 5, WORLD.height - unit.radius - 5);
              if (this.findBuildingCollision(px, py, unit.radius)) continue;
              if (this.resources.some((resource) => resource.alive && Math.hypot(resource.x - px, resource.y - py) < resource.radius + unit.radius + 8)) continue;
              if ((this.decorations || []).some((feature) => feature.type === 'rock' && feature.radius >= 13 && Math.hypot(feature.x - px, feature.y - py) < feature.radius + unit.radius + 3)) continue;
              if (reserved.some((entry) => Math.hypot(entry.x - px, entry.y - py) < entry.radius + unit.radius + 7)) continue;
              slot = { x: px, y: py, radius: unit.radius };
              break outer;
            }
          }
        }
        if (!slot) continue;
        const oldX = unit.x;
        const oldY = unit.y;
        unit.x = slot.x;
        unit.y = slot.y;
        unit.renderX = oldX;
        unit.renderY = oldY;
        unit.lastPositionX = slot.x;
        unit.lastPositionY = slot.y;
        unit.navRecoveryPoint = null;
        unit.navRecoveryUntil = 0;
        unit.invalidateNavigation();
        reserved.push(slot);
        moved += 1;
        this.addEffect({ type: 'marker', x: slot.x, y: slot.y, color: unit.team === 'player' ? '#8fe6b2' : '#f0a078', duration: 0.45 });
      }
      if (moved) {
        this.rebuildSpatialIndexes();
        this.resolveUnitOverlaps(3);
      }
      return moved;
    }

      for (const unit of stillInside) this.resolveUnitBuildingOverlap(unit);
      if (stillInside.length) {
        this.rebuildSpatialIndexes();
        this.resolveUnitOverlaps(2);
      }
      const workers = workerIds.map((id) => this.getEntity(id)).filter((worker) => worker?.alive && worker.typeId === 'worker');
      for (const worker of workers) worker.setCommand({ type: 'build', buildingId: building.id }, append);
      this.addEffect({ type: 'marker', x, y, color: '#8fe6b2', duration: 0.8 });

      this.sound.build();

      else if (this.buildMode) this.buildMode.rotation = rotation;
      this.recalculatePower();
      this.uiDirty = true;
      return true;
    }

    cancelModes() {
      this.commandMode = null;
      this.buildMode = null;
      this.input.buildPlacementDrag = null;
      this.uiDirty = true;
    }

    sellSelectedBuilding() {
      const building = this.selected.length === 1 ? this.selected[0] : null;
      if (!building || building.kind !== 'building' || building.team !== 'player' || building.typeId === 'hq' || building.typeId === 'oil') return;
      const refund = building.stats.cost * (building.completed ? 0.5 : 0.75 * building.construction);
      this.teams.player.credits += refund;
      building.die(null);
      this.addEffect({ type: 'text', x: building.x, y: building.y - 24, text: `+${Math.round(refund)}`, color: '#ffe083', duration: 1.3 });
      this.clearSelection();
    }


    strategicTypeForConfig(config) {
      return Object.entries(POWER_TYPES).find(([, candidate]) => candidate === config)?.[0] || null;
    }

    findStrategicLaunchers(teamKey, type, readyOnly = false) {
      const config = POWER_TYPES[type];
      if (!config?.strategic) return [];
      return this.units.filter((unit) => {
        if (!unit.alive || unit.team !== teamKey || !unit.stats.strategicLauncher?.includes(type)) return false;
        if (readyOnly && (unit.munitionCooldowns[type] || 0) > 0) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }

    powerRequirementMet(teamKey, config) {
      if (!config?.strategic) return true;
      const type = this.strategicTypeForConfig(config);
      return Boolean(type && this.findStrategicLaunchers(teamKey, type, false).length);
    }

    launcherRangeStatus(launcher, config, x, y) {
      const range = Math.hypot(launcher.x - x, launcher.y - y);
      return { range, valid: range >= (config.minRange || 0) && range <= (config.maxRange || Infinity) };
    }

    findStrategicLauncher(teamKey, type, x, y, preferredLauncherId = null, readyOnly = true) {
      const config = POWER_TYPES[type];
      if (!config?.strategic) return null;
      // Явно выбранный носитель никогда не подменяется другой установкой.
      // Поэтому несколько платформ имеют независимые позиции и перезарядки.
      if (preferredLauncherId) {
        const preferred = this.getEntity(preferredLauncherId);
        if (!preferred?.alive || preferred.kind !== 'unit' || preferred.team !== teamKey || !preferred.stats.strategicLauncher?.includes(type)) return null;
        if (readyOnly && (preferred.munitionCooldowns[type] || 0) > 0) return null;
        return this.launcherRangeStatus(preferred, config, x, y).valid ? preferred : null;
      }
      const candidates = this.findStrategicLaunchers(teamKey, type, readyOnly)
        .filter((unit) => this.launcherRangeStatus(unit, config, x, y).valid);
      if (!candidates.length) return null;
      return candidates.sort((a, b) => {

    activatePower(type) {
      const config = POWER_TYPES[type];
      const team = this.teams.player;
      if (!config || team.rank < config.rank) return;
      if (config.strategic) {
        this.activateLauncherWeapon(type);
        return;
      }
      if (team.powers[type] > 0) return;
      if (type === 'orbital' && !this.hasBuilding('player', 'uplink', true)) {
        this.alert('Требуется стратегический ретранслятор', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      this.buildMode = null;
      this.commandMode = `power:${type}`;
      this.alert(`Выберите цель: ${config.name}`, 'info');
      this.uiDirty = true;
    }

    executePower(type, x, y) {
      const config = POWER_TYPES[type];
      const team = this.teams.player;
      if (!config || team.powers[type] > 0 || team.rank < config.rank) return false;
      if (config.strategic) return this.launchStrategicWeapon(type, x, y, 'player');
      if (type === 'orbital' && !this.hasBuilding('player', 'uplink', true)) return false;
      team.powers[type] = config.cooldown;
      if (type === 'scan') this.abilityZones.push(new AbilityZone(this, { type: 'scan', team: 'player', x, y, radius: 620, duration: 12 }));
      else if (type === 'repair') this.abilityZones.push(new AbilityZone(this, { type: 'repair', team: 'player', x, y, radius: 300, duration: 10 }));
      else if (type === 'artillery') this.abilityZones.push(new AbilityZone(this, { type: 'artillery', team: 'player', x, y, radius: 260, duration: 6.0 }));
      else if (type === 'orbital') this.abilityZones.push(new AbilityZone(this, { type: 'orbital', team: 'player', x, y, radius: 340, duration: 6.2 }));
      this.commandMode = null;
      this.uiDirty = true;
      this.sound.alert();
      return true;
    }

    executeCurrentMode(x, y, append = false) {
      if (this.buildMode) return this.placeBuilding(x, y, append, this.buildMode.rotation);
      if (this.commandMode?.startsWith('launcher:')) {
        const [, type, launcherId] = this.commandMode.split(':');
        const launched = this.launchStrategicWeapon(type, x, y, 'player', launcherId || null);
        if (launched) this.commandMode = null;
        return launched;
      }
      if (this.commandMode?.startsWith('power:')) return this.executePower(this.commandMode.split(':')[1], x, y);
      return this.executeCommandMode(x, y, append);
    }

    save(notify = true) {
      if (this.ended) return false;
      try {
        const data = {
          version: GAME_VERSION,
          seed: this.seed,
          time: this.time,
          idCounter: this.idCounter,
          difficultyKey: this.difficultyKey,
          camera: this.camera,
          objective: this.objective,
          stats: this.stats,
          teams: {
            player: this.serializeTeam(this.teams.player),
            enemy: this.serializeTeam(this.teams.enemy)
          },
          entities: [...this.units, ...this.buildings, ...this.resources].filter((entity) => entity.alive).map((entity) => entity.serialize()),
          abilityZones: this.abilityZones.map((zone) => zone.serialize()),
          spyCells: this.spyCells.map((cell) => ({ ...cell })),
          spyCellCounter: this.spyCellCounter,
          projectileCounter: this.projectileCounter,
          explored: Array.from(this.explored),
          scorchMarks: this.scorchMarks.slice(-80),
          selectedIds: this.selected.map((entity) => entity.id),
          controlGroups: this.controlGroups,
          ai: this.ai?.serialize() || null,
          savedAt: Date.now()
        };
        storageSet(SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
        DOM.loadButton.disabled = false;
        if (notify) this.alert('Игра сохранена', 'info');
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(error);
        if (notify) this.alert('Не удалось сохранить игру', 'danger');
        return false;
      }
    }

    }

  // Use the standard placement command. This keeps costs, unit evacuation,

  Game.prototype.buildExtractorFromResource83 = function(node) {
    const typeId = extractorTypeFor(node?.variant);
    if (!node?.alive || node.kind !== 'resource' || !typeId) return false;
    if (!extractorNodeAvailable(this, node)) {
      this.alert('На этом месторождении уже работает добывающее предприятие.', 'warning', node.x, node.y);
      return false;
    }
    const stats = getBuildingStats(typeId, this.teams.player);
    const workers = this.units
      .filter((unit) => unit.alive && unit.team === 'player' && unit.typeId === 'worker' && !unit.embarkedIn)
      .sort((left, right) => distance(left, node) - distance(right, node));
    if (!workers.length) {
      this.alert('Нужен хотя бы один свободный инженер для строительства добывающего предприятия.', 'warning', node.x, node.y);
      return false;
    }
    if (!this.requirementsMet('player', stats.requires || [], stats.rank || 1)) {
      this.alert('Сначала постройте необходимую энергетическую и технологическую инфраструктуру.', 'warning', node.x, node.y);
      return false;
    }
    if (this.teams.player.credits < stats.cost) {
      this.alert('Недостаточно ресурсов для строительства добывающего предприятия.', 'warning', node.x, node.y);
      return false;
    }
    const rotation = Math.atan2((this.playerBase?.y ?? node.y) - node.y, (this.playerBase?.x ?? node.x + 1) - node.x);
    if (!this.isBuildPlacementValid(typeId, node.x, node.y, rotation, node)) {
      this.alert('Контур месторождения заблокирован другим зданием или рельефом.', 'warning', node.x, node.y);
      return false;
    }

    const previousMode = this.buildMode;
    this.buildMode = { typeId, workerIds: workers.slice(0, 3).map((worker) => worker.id), rotation };
    const placed = this.placeBuilding(node.x, node.y, false, rotation);
    if (this.buildMode?.typeId === typeId) this.buildMode = previousMode || null;
    if (!placed) return false;

    const building = node.extractorBuildingId ? this.getEntity(node.extractorBuildingId) : null;
    if (building?.alive) this.setSelection([building], false);
    this.uiDirty = true;
    return true;
  };

    if (node?.kind !== 'resource' || !actionButtons || !queuePanel) return result;

    const typeId = extractorTypeFor(node.variant);
    const stats = typeId ? getBuildingStats(typeId, this.teams.player) : null;

    actionButtons.replaceChildren();

      ? `${existing.completed ? 'добыча запущена' : `строительство ${Math.round((existing.construction || 0) * 100)}%`}`
      : stats ? `требуется: ${stats.name}` : 'тип месторождения не поддерживается';
    card.innerHTML = `<div class="resource-site-icon">${presentation.icon}</div><div><strong>${presentation.name}</strong><small>Запас: ${Math.round(node.amount).toLocaleString('ru-RU')} · ${status}</small></div>`;
    actionButtons.appendChild(card);
    if (!stats) return result;

    const workerCount = this.units.filter((unit) => unit.alive && unit.team === 'player' && unit.typeId === 'worker' && !unit.embarkedIn).length;
    const unlocked = this.requirementsMet('player', stats.requires || [], stats.rank || 1);
    const affordable = this.teams.player.credits >= stats.cost;
    const button = document.createElement('button');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.className = 'resource-build-button';
    button.disabled = hasExtractor || !workerCount || !unlocked || !affordable;
    button.textContent = hasExtractor
      ? `✓ ${stats.name}: ${existing.completed ? 'работает' : 'строится'}`
      : `Построить: ${stats.name} · ${Math.round(stats.cost).toLocaleString('ru-RU')} ¤`;
    button.title = hasExtractor
      ? 'На этом месторождении уже есть добывающее предприятие.'
      : !workerCount ? 'Нет доступных инженеров.'
      : !unlocked ? 'Не выполнены технологические требования.'
      : !affordable ? 'Недостаточно ресурсов.'
      : 'Установить точный фундамент на месторождении и направить до трёх ближайших инженеров.';
    button.addEventListener('click', () => this.buildExtractorFromResource83(node));
    actionButtons.appendChild(button);
    return result;
  };

  // The opponent uses the same six-way mapping and the same physical logistics.
  TacticalAI.prototype.ensureExtractors80 = function() {
    const game = this.game;
    const team = game.teams.enemy;
    if (!team) return;
    const nodes = game.resources
      .filter((node) => extractorNodeAvailable(game, node) && distance(node, this.base) < 2300)
      .sort((left, right) => distance(left, this.base) - distance(right, this.base));
    for (const node of nodes) {
      const typeId = extractorTypeFor(node.variant);
      const stats = getBuildingStats(typeId, team);
      if (team.credits < stats.cost || !game.requirementsMet('enemy', stats.requires || [], stats.rank || 1)) continue;
      const rotation = this.ensureBasePlan79 ? this.ensureBasePlan79().heading : Math.PI;
      if (!game.isBuildPlacementValid(typeId, node.x, node.y, rotation, node, 'enemy')) continue;
      const building = new Building(game, {
        typeId, team: 'enemy', x: node.x, y: node.y, rotation, weaponRotation: rotation,
        construction: 0.03, autoConstruct: true,
      });
      building.resourceNodeId = node.id;
      building.resourceBuffer83 = 0;
      building.extractTimer83 = 0;
      node.extractorBuildingId = building.id;
      team.credits -= stats.cost;
      game.addEntity(building);
      break;
    }
  };

    considerProduction() {
      this.ensureStrategicPlatforms();
      const game = this.game;
      const team = game.teams.enemy;
      const composition = this.lastPlayerComposition;
      const army = game.units.filter((unit) => unit.alive && unit.team === 'enemy' && unit.typeId !== 'worker');
      const roleCounts = {
        scout: army.filter((unit) => this.isScout(unit)).length,
        siege: army.filter((unit) => this.isSiege(unit)).length,
        support: army.filter((unit) => this.isSupport(unit)).length,
        antiAir: army.filter((unit) => this.isAntiAir(unit)).length,
        covert: army.filter((unit) => this.isCovert(unit)).length,
        total: army.length

      const producers = game.buildings.filter((building) => building.alive && building.team === 'enemy' && building.completed && building.queue.length < (game.difficultyKey === 'hard' ? 3 : 2) && building.stats.produces?.length);

      for (const building of producers) {
        if (building.typeId === 'hq' || building.typeId === 'forwardOutpost') continue;
        let candidates = building.stats.produces.filter((id) => game.canProduceUnit('enemy', id));
        candidates = candidates.filter((id) => (UNIT_TYPES[id].tier || UNIT_TYPES[id].rank || 1) <= maxTier || ['rifle', 'rocket', 'medic', 'scout', 'tank', 'aa', 'helicopter'].includes(id));
        if (!candidates.length) continue;
        const scored = candidates.map((id) => ({ id, score: this.productionScore(id, composition, roleCounts, team.credits) }))
          .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
        const choicePool = scored.slice(0, Math.min(3, scored.length));
        const chosen = choicePool[Math.floor(this.random() * choicePool.length)]?.id;
        if (chosen) game.queueProduction(building, chosen, 'unit', true);
      }

      const researchBuildings = game.buildings.filter((building) => building.alive && building.team === 'enemy' && building.completed && building.queue.length === 0 && building.stats.researches?.length);
      const priorities = this.researchPriorities(composition);
      for (const lab of researchBuildings) {
        const missing = lab.stats.researches.filter((id) => !team.upgrades.has(id));
        if (!missing.length) continue;
        missing.sort((a, b) => priorities.indexOf(a) - priorities.indexOf(b));
        game.queueProduction(lab, missing[0], 'upgrade', true);
      }
    }

    findAiBuildSpot(typeId, base, salt = 0) {
      const stats = getBuildingStats(typeId, this.teams.enemy);
      for (let i = 0; i < 48; i += 1) {
        const ring = 150 + Math.floor((i + salt) / 10) * 72;
        const angle = (i * 2.399 + salt * 0.77) % TWO_PI;
        const x = clamp(base.x + Math.cos(angle) * ring, stats.radius + 20, WORLD.width - stats.radius - 20);
        const y = clamp(base.y + Math.sin(angle) * ring, stats.radius + 20, WORLD.height - stats.radius - 20);
        if (!this.collidesWithBuilding(x, y, stats.radius + 12)) return { x, y };
      }
      return null;
    }

    hasBuilding(team, typeId, completedOnly = true) {
      return this.buildings.some((building) => building.alive && building.team === team && building.typeId === typeId && (!completedOnly || building.completed));
    }

    requirementsMet(team, requirements = [], rank = 1) {
      if (this.teams[team].rank < rank) return false;
      return requirements.every((typeId) => this.hasBuilding(team, typeId, true));
    }

    canProduceUnit(team, typeId) {
      const base = UNIT_TYPES[typeId];
      if (!base) return false;
      if (base.faction && base.faction !== this.teams[team].faction) return false;
      return this.requirementsMet(team, base.requires || [], base.rank || 1);
    }

    queueProduction(building, itemId, kind = 'unit', silent = false) {
      if (!building || !building.alive || !building.completed || building.queue.length >= 10) return false;
      const team = this.teams[building.team];
      let cost;
      let time;
      let name;
      if (kind === 'unit') {
        if (!building.stats.produces?.includes(itemId) || !this.canProduceUnit(building.team, itemId)) return false;
        const stats = getUnitStats(itemId, team);
        cost = stats.cost;
        time = stats.time;
        name = stats.name;
      } else {
        const upgrade = UPGRADES[itemId];
        if (!upgrade || !building.stats.researches?.includes(itemId) || team.upgrades.has(itemId) || building.queue.some((item) => item.kind === 'upgrade' && item.id === itemId)) return false;
        cost = upgrade.cost;
        time = upgrade.time;
        name = upgrade.name;
      }
      if (team.credits < cost) {
        if (!silent && building.team === 'player') this.alert('Недостаточно ресурсов', 'warning');
        return false;
      }
      team.credits -= cost;
      building.queue.push({ kind, id: itemId, remaining: time, total: time, cost, name });
      this.uiDirty = true;
      if (!silent) this.sound.click();
      return true;
    }

    cancelQueueItem(building, index) {
      if (!building || index < 0 || index >= building.queue.length) return;
      const [item] = building.queue.splice(index, 1);
      this.teams[building.team].credits += item.cost * 0.75;
      this.uiDirty = true;
    }

    completeUpgrade(team, upgradeId) {
      const state = this.teams[team];
      if (state.upgrades.has(upgradeId)) return;
      state.upgrades.add(upgradeId);
      for (const unit of this.units) {
        if (unit.team !== team || !unit.alive) continue;
        const oldMax = unit.maxHp;
        const oldRatio = unit.hp / oldMax;
        unit.stats = getUnitStats(unit.typeId, state);
        unit.maxHp = unit.stats.hp * (1 + (unit.rank - 1) * 0.12);
        if (upgradeId === 'armor' && unit.vehicle) unit.hp = Math.max(unit.hp, unit.maxHp * oldRatio);
        else unit.hp = Math.min(unit.hp, unit.maxHp);
      }
      for (const building of this.buildings) {
        if (building.team !== team || !building.alive || building.typeId === 'oil') continue;
        const oldRatio = building.hp / building.maxHp;
        building.stats = getBuildingStats(building.typeId, state);
        building.maxHp = building.stats.hp;
        if (upgradeId === 'fortification') building.hp = Math.max(building.hp, building.maxHp * oldRatio);
        else building.hp = Math.min(building.hp, building.maxHp);
      }
      if (team === 'player') this.alert(`${UPGRADES[upgradeId].name}: исследование завершено`, 'info');
      this.addCommandXp(team, 300);
      this.uiDirty = true;
    }

    recalculatePower() {
      for (const teamKey of ['player', 'enemy']) {
        let produced = 0;
        let used = 0;
        for (const building of this.buildings) {
          if (!building.alive || !building.completed || building.team !== teamKey) continue;
          if (building.sabotagedUntil > this.time) continue;
          produced += building.stats.power || 0;
          used += building.stats.powerUse || 0;
        }
        const team = this.teams[teamKey];
        team.powerProduced = produced;
        team.powerUsed = used;
        team.powerFactor = used <= produced || used === 0 ? 1 : clamp(produced / used, 0.22, 1);
      }
    }

    updatePowerCooldowns(dt) {
      for (const teamKey of ['player', 'enemy']) {
        const powers = this.teams[teamKey].powers;
        const cooldownFactor = this.getStrategicCooldownFactor(teamKey);
        for (const key of Object.keys(powers)) powers[key] = Math.max(0, powers[key] - dt / cooldownFactor);
      }
    }

    addCommandXp(team, amount) {
      if (!this.teams[team] || team === 'neutral' || amount <= 0) return;
      const state = this.teams[team];
      state.commandXp += amount;
      const oldRank = state.rank;
      if (state.commandXp >= COMMAND_RANK_THRESHOLDS[2]) state.rank = 3;
      else if (state.commandXp >= COMMAND_RANK_THRESHOLDS[1]) state.rank = 2;
      if (state.rank > oldRank && team === 'player') {
        this.alert(`Командный ранг повышен до ${state.rank}`, 'info');
        this.sound.alert();
      }
      this.uiDirty = true;
    }

    addEffect(effect) {
      this.effects.push({ age: 0, duration: effect.duration || 0.5, ...effect });
    }
